Title: A Serious Call to a Devout and Holy Life, adapted to the State and Condition of All Orders of Christians
Creator: William Law, A.M.
Publisher: London: printed for William Innys, at the West End of St. Paul's
Publication Date: 1729
Rights: Public Domain (published 1729, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'NOT_IN_COPYRIGHT')
Source: Internet Archive item seriouscalltode00laww (https://archive.org/details/seriouscalltode00laww), the item's own seriouscalltode00laww_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Princeton Theological Seminary Library; host metadata date: 1729. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 07270ea9fe87fb0feec9037053e660fa3e26198355d5b8a3475771258c11d4f0.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Princeton Theological Seminary Library scan of the first edition; good OCR with the long s kept.
Content note: Law's call to a devotion that governs every hour of an ordinary life, in twenty-four chapters with their character sketches (Flavia, Miranda and the rest) and the chapters on prayer at set hours, at twelve o'clock and in the evening among them; written by a nonjuror outside the parish ministry.
